### 2. Decisions taken beyond the specification

1. **`oci_topology.ATTACHMENT_STYLE_MODES` duplicates `drawio_builder.ATTACHMENT_STYLES`.**
   `parse_terraform.py` must be able to validate `attachment_style` without importing the
   builder (importing it registers 159 SVGs; the parser only imports it lazily in
   `_builder_icon_keys()`). The two tuples are pinned together by
   `ViewModeTests.test_attachment_style_modes_match_the_builder` (3.1), the same pattern the repo
   already uses for `HUB_KINDS`.
2. **Two new builder primitives**: `DrawioBuilder.move(cid, dx, dy)` (5.4) and
   `DrawioBuilder.reparent(cid, new_parent)` (7.4). `resize(x=, y=)` cannot move an icon - the
   slot the layout reasons about is recorded separately and would stay behind - and B04/B05/B06
   all need cells to change parent after they are laid out. Both are private-by-convention
   additions used only by the recipe; neither is exported.
3. **The re-centring pass translates every region child by half the growth** (5.8) rather than
   re-centring the DRG column and the on-premises panel individually. Growing the region adds the
   height at the bottom, so one uniform translation keeps the VCN stack - and everything already
   centred on it - centred, with no relation inside the region changing. Decision 6's wording
   ("re-centred on the region's final VCN stack") is satisfied exactly, and the pass is provably
   a no-op when nothing grew, which is what keeps `locations: "nested"` byte-identical.
4. **`_view_ctx` is the single source of the view** (4.5). Every later task reads `ctx[...]`
   instead of re-reading the model, so a keyword argument, a model key and a CLI flag cannot
   diverge. `layout_info` gains `locations` (4.9), `canvas` (5.10) and `compartments` (7.8).
5. **`GW_ICON_TYPES` / `_gateway_type`** (4.5): the spec's gateway table is written in terms of
   IGW / NAT / SGW / LPG, but a hand-written model may give only an icon. One table maps icon
   keys to types and is the only place that inference lives.
6. **`VCN_Y_TOP_GW = 110`** (6.3) is not in the spec. A top-border gateway's slot starts
   `GW_STRADDLE` above the VCN and its caption a further `LABEL_GAP + LABEL_H` above that, so the
   caption's top is `vcn_y - 87`; with the 1.3.0 `VCN_Y = 40` it lands outside the region.
   `PAD + 87 = 107`, rounded up to the ten-pixel grid, is 110 - the smallest value that keeps the
   caption inside the region's padding.
7. **`_compartment_gap`** (7.6): the spec fixes `CMP_PAD = 30` and `CMP_GAP = 40` but not the
   column gap between two VCNs in different compartments. `VCN_COLUMN_GAP = 45` is smaller than
   the two paddings, so the containers would overlap - a hard `OVERLAP:` error. The gap grows by
   the borders that actually close and open between the two columns, which also handles nesting.
8. **Compartments are drawn after the columns and their members re-parented** (7.7), instead of
   laying each VCN out inside a pre-created compartment. The container is then fitted to what it
   really holds (including the gateways straddling the VCN border), and `_layout_vcn` /
   `_place_edge_gateway` keep one coordinate space. The spec's "`region_id` is renamed
   `parent_id`" is still done (6.5), because Task 7 hands `_place_edge_gateway` a compartment id
   in the general case.
9. **The two-line title markup** (4.6) is
   `name<br><font style="font-size: 10px" color="#312D2A">cidr</font>` with `raw_html=True`.
   Slide 18 specifies "Text2: 9pt, light, Bark"; 10 px against the 11 px title is the plugin's
   rendering of that, and Bark is the spec's colour. Validator rule 5b already counts `<br>`
   paragraphs, so a two-line title does not trip the badge-width warning.
10. **Legend texts** (4.8): `Route table`, `Security list`, `Network security group`,
    `DRG route table`, and `Attachment / association (structural)` for the dotted form. These are
    contractual - tests assert them.
11. **`GROUP_SCOPES = ("subnet", "vcn")`** (3.5) is shared by `normalise_groups` and the parser's
    `new_group(..., scope=)` (10.4), so the member key (`items` on a subnet, `subnets` on a VCN)
    has exactly one definition.
12. **No new `oci` CLI call for a live tenancy** (10.11). Cluster, node-pool and compartment
    entities already arrive through the structured search, so the OKE box and the compartment
    names come for free through the shared `ModelBuilder`; `_REF_FIELDS` gains
    `drg_route_table_id` and `cluster_id`, and the compartment parent chain stays authored.
13. **The reference sample gains an `internet` box but no Internet Gateway** (11.2). Spec section
    9 says "an Internet box receives the IGW", but `Spoke-VCN-D` has no IGW - inventing one would
    change the canonical architecture. With the Internet box present the NAT takes the
    Internet-facing border and the SGW moves to the bottom facing the OSN band, which is the
    v1.4.0 geometry the sample is meant to show.
14. **`smoke_test.sh` learns to look in `~/Applications`** (13.6), so the full smoke test passes
    without an explicit `DRAWIO_BIN`. `drawio_builder.find_drawio_binary()` already probes there;
    the script did not, and no other task owned the gap.
15. **Every task that moves the sample regenerates and commits it inside that task** (4.13,
    5.12, 6.10, and the `cmp` checks in 7.10, 8.8, 9.9 for the tasks whose features are off by
    default). The suite is therefore green after every task and no task inherits another's red
    test; the cost is three extra regenerations of a tracked file, which is what v1.3.1 did too.
    Task 11 regenerates only the four README images.
16. **The G4 Internet-facing border depends on the column** (6.4): `right` for the rightmost VCN
    column, `top` for every other one, because a left-hand column's right border faces the next
    VCN rather than the Internet box. `gateway_edge` overrides both.
17. **Every Internet Gateway gets an attachment connector to the Internet box** (6.7), mirroring
    the Service Gateway's connector to the Oracle Services Network. Nothing is drawn when there
    is no Internet box.
18. **The hybrid label may cross the region border in `drg_style="box"`** (5.11). G3 requires the
    label not to overlap a shape, and it does not; fully centring it on the gap segment alone
    needs a router change (the label is centred on the whole horizontal run, which starts inside
    the On-Premises box). Recorded as a follow-up, not shipped in 1.4.0.

### 3. Placeholder scan

No step says "TBD", "similar to Task N", "add appropriate handling" or "write tests for the
above". Every code step quotes the exact text to find and the exact text to write; every test
step carries the full test body; every run step gives the command and the expected output; every
commit step gives the command. Three steps allow for a value that only the machine can supply,
and each names the command that produces it and the field to read:

- 12.13 and 13.7, the generated-file-size and packaging-size sentences (the demo grew a page and
  the reference changed canvas) - each step gives the command and the output field to read.
- 13.9, the visual PNG check - the step lists the acceptance criteria to check the rendering
  against, one per spec rule.
- 13.11, the conditional final commit - it exists only if a gate found something, and the step
  says what to write in the body and which fix is committed there by default.

`grep -n "TBD\|TODO\|FIXME\|\.\.\.\s*$\|<one line\|as appropriate" ` over this plan returns only
the `...` inside quoted expected outputs (`Ran N tests ... OK`, `OK: no container overlaps or
layout errors (...)`), which is literal CLI output, and the `...` inside quoted Python argument
lists that the surrounding step spells out in full.

### 4. Type consistency

- **`ctx`** is produced only by `_view_ctx` (4.5) and has exactly the keys `locations: str`,
  `gateway_edge: str`, `subnet_label: str`, `attachment_style: Optional[str]`,
  `show_compartments: bool`, `internet: Optional[dict]`, `third_party: List[dict]`,
  `has_internet: bool`. `_gateway_side` (6.4) reads it with `.get`, so the 1.3.1 three-argument
  calls (`ctx=None`) keep working; `_layout_locations` (5.8) and `_layout_right_column` (5.8)
  read it by key.
- **`attachment_style`** is `None` (take the profile) or one of `ATTACHMENT_STYLE_MODES`
  everywhere: `oci_topology.attachment_style_of` returns exactly that, `build_diagram` passes it
  to `DrawioBuilder(attachment_style=)`, which resolves it to a non-empty `str` on
  `self.attachment_style`, which `_kind_style` and `_legend_entries` read. No other spelling
  exists.
- **`groups[]`** has one normaliser: `oci_topology.normalise_groups` returns
  `{"type": str, "label": str, "members": List[str], "key": str}` and is the only producer;
  `_layout_group_boxes` (8.4) is the only consumer. The member key per scope
  (`items` / `subnets`) is defined once, in `_GROUP_MEMBER_KEYS` keyed by `GROUP_SCOPES`, and the
  parser's `new_group(..., scope=)` (10.4) writes through the same table. Every `type` value in
  `GROUP_BOX_TYPES` is also in `drawio_builder.GROUP_TYPES` (`other` was already there; Task 1
  adds the three new ones), so `add_group(group_type=entry["type"])` can never raise.
- **`route_table`** is `str | dict | list | None` on a subnet and on a DRG, and every reader goes
  through `oci_topology.badge_refs()` - `oci_layout._badge_refs` is an alias of it (3.9), and
  `drg_route_tables()` is `badge_refs()` on `drg["route_table"]`. The parser writes a **list**
  (10.7) and `validate_model` accepts all four shapes (10.5). `DRG_RT_MAX = 2` is defined once,
  in `oci_layout` (9.3), and is the only cap.
- **`reg.badge_kinds`** holds exactly the four strings `"route_table"`, `"security_list"`,
  `"nsg"`, `"drg_route_table"`, written in 4.8 and 9.5 and read only by `_legend_entries`'s
  `LEGEND_BADGE_ROWS` table, whose first column uses the same four spellings.
- **`member_cells`** (7.8) is keyed by VCN **name**, which is what `compartment_tree` node
  `"vcns"` entries hold (3.7 fills them from `compartments[].vcns` and from `vcn["compartment"]`,
  both name-based), and what `_compartment_chain` looks up. A VCN without a name maps to `""`
  and is never claimed.
- **Cell ids**: `hub`, `internet`, `thirdparty-<n>`, `compartment-<name>`, `tenancy`,
  `<drg address>-rt` / `<drg address>-rt2`, and a group box's `groups[].key` or
  `group:<parent key>:<type>:<slug>`. All go through `_new_id(key)` and therefore `_slug()`, so
  `compartment:Network` is written as `compartment-Network` - which is what the Task 7 tests
  assert and what the Task 12 docs state.
- **`layout_info`** stays a `dict` with the 1.3.1 keys `topology`, `warnings`, `drg_style` plus
  `locations` (4.9), `canvas` (5.10) and `compartments` (7.8). `warnings` stays `List[str]` and
  is the same list `_layout_drg_column` appends the route-table overflow warning to (9.7).
- **`DrawioBuilder.move` / `reparent`** operate on the builder's own registry and the emitted
  XML together; `reparent` moves an icon's caption with it and refuses a parent that is inside
  the cell, so no ancestor cycle can be created and `_origin()` can never loop.
